# Competitor coverage report

Generated by `scripts/competitor-coverage.mjs`. Each row is one verbatim expression from a corpus file; `supported` means the calculator evaluated it and the result matched the expected display value, `different` means it evaluated to another value (formatting or semantic conflict), `unsupported` means it was rejected.

- Binary: `target/release/link-calculator`
- Corpus: `docs/case-studies/issue-227/corpus`
- Timeout per expression: 5000 ms
